  Resolve the Deadeye Navigator trigger, repairing it and Exchange of Words.

## Combo 2724-2963-3080-4608
Cards: Exchange of Words + Starfield of Nyx + Wormfang Manta + Teleportation Circle
Color identity: WU | Bracket: Ruthless | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {1}{U}{U}
Produces: Infinite turns; Lock
Start: Exchange of Words (hand); Starfield of Nyx (battlefield); Wormfang Manta (battlefield); Teleportation Circle (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You control at least four enchantments.
Steps: Cast Exchange of Words by paying {1}{U}{U}.
  When Exchange of Words enters the battlefield, it triggers, exchanging the text boxes of it and Wormfang Manta.
  At the beginning of your end step, Teleportation Circle triggers, blinking Exchange of Words.
  Exchange of Words leaves and enters the battlefield, triggering the second ability it gained from Wormfang Manta's textbox as well as its own ability.
  Resolve the first Exchange of Words trigger, causing you to take an extra turn after this one.
  Resolve the second Exchange of Words trigger, exchanging the text boxes of it and Wormfang Manta.
  Repeat from step 3 during each turn.

Start: Exchange of Words (hand); Opalescence (battlefield); Wormfang Manta (battlefield); The Mind Stone (battlefield: is harnessed)
Steps: Cast Exchange of Words by paying {1}{U}{U}.
  When Exchange of Words enters the battlefield, it triggers, exchanging the text boxes of it and Wormfang Manta.
  At the beginning of your end step, The Mind Stone triggers, blinking Exchange of Words.
  Exchange of Words leaves and enters the battlefield, triggering the second ability it gained from Wormfang Manta's textbox as well as its own ability.
  Resolve the first Exchange of Words trigger, causing you to take an extra turn after this one.
  Resolve the second Exchange of Words trigger, exchanging the text boxes of it and Wormfang Manta.
  Repeat from step 3 during each turn.

  Repeat from step 4.

  Clever Conjurer is exiled by Agatha's Soul Cauldron.
